# Run 3 extended tunnel levels - flat tile bitmaps.
# '.' = solid tile   '#' = void   'c' = crumbling (solid, falls
# when the runner steps on it, then the connected group follows).
# Each [level] block is one checkpoint: rows top to bottom, one
# line per row, index = side*k + lane (side major), matching the
# bit layout in levels_baked.h. Rows 0-3 read solid in the engine.
# Tunnel ids match levels.c. Do not regenerate from a script -
# this IS the authored source: edit a level by editing its rows.

[path=wormholeSpace n=16 k=1 levels=12]
